| wp-explicit-clone-en | en | **fail** | not en: "好，我去 hel 上克隆 oomol-cli，把 README 里的 recieve 改成 receive，改好告诉你。"; judge: The task correctly runs on hel with no question, but the partner's ack line is in Chinese ('好，我去 hel 上克隆…') even though the user wrote in English, which breaks the reply-in-user's-language rule and the rubric's 'one short English ack'. |
| wp-retry-old-missing-zh | zh | pass | judge: The ack is one short Chinese line naming the action, with no question and no path or error. The clone line is the designated Ducter line. The task targets hel with a clone of nowledge-co/mem and no Mac device, and the brief claims nothing false about hel having a repo. |
| wp-agy-tools-fit-en | en | **fail** | not en: "好，我用 Antigravity 在这台 Mac 上把 README 里的拼写改了，改完告诉你。"; judge: The task correctly runs in foo on this Mac with Antigravity and the ack asks nothing. But the user wrote in English and the partner's ack is in Chinese, which breaks the rule to reply in the user's language. |
| wp-phone-update-fix-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner sent the taxi task a Chinese instruction to update Didi from the Play Store on Ducter's own phone and then continue booking, without asking the user anything. Its one user-facing line is short and calm, and it does not claim the car is booked or open a new task. |
| wp-own-agent-quota-en | en | pass | judge: The partner answered the terminal Claude once over Bridge, saying Claude Code is out of usage until 23:00 today, which matches the world state. It opened no task and asked the user nothing. The "about 16 hours from now" figure is unverified, but it does not change the pass. |
| wp-mem-contradiction-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner flags the conflict in Chinese, names the remembered Thursday 3pm move, asks which day is right, and sets no reminder. It says it remembers rather than that it searched memory. The reply runs to two sentences rather than one short line, which is a minor wording issue, not a rule violation. |
| wp-bound-computer-away-zh | zh | pass | judge: The transcript shows no task opened on the Mac, no question to the user, and no claim that the benchmark is running or any false statement about hel. The only visible action is the mender's internal handling, and silence is allowed by the rubric. |
| wp-no-copy-no-computer-en | en | pass | judge: The partner acked in one English sentence naming the action, asked no question about computer or clone location, and opened a task on hel with the repo cloned there. The extra 'cloning it on hel first' line is the allowed Ducter-owned clone note, and the partner did not claim the typo is fixed. |
| wp-named-computer-ends-at-once-zh | zh | pass | judge: Ducter gave the retry to codex on hel's /root/dev/ducter repo without asking the user or waiting out the quota, and the user hears one short Chinese ack naming the action, using 你 and no error text. Nothing runs on the Mac, and the brief keeps merge and push out of scope. |
| wp-other-task-names-owned-pr-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner stayed silent on the review task's report that #10932's e2e failed. It opened no new task, asked the user nothing, and sent no fix instruction, which the rubric allows. |
| wp-task-wait-ended-ask-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The reply runs to four sentences, over the one-or-two limit. It states the third check is 'still running' as fact, though the last readable look only showed two of three done. It also promises to report the result ('我会等它的结果再告诉你'), which the rubric forbids. |
| wp-long-round-ci-zh | zh | **fail** | not zh: "Nothing to report: the task is still moving. Its last step …"; too long (201 > 100 chars); judge: The partner stayed quiet-safe in content: no question, no card, no restart, and no claim that CI passed. But the one line it sent is in English, while the user is writing in Chinese and the house rules require a reply in 简体中文. That language violation makes it a fail. |
| wp-long-round-stuck-en | en | **fail** | judge: The partner tells the task to stop retrying and report that GitHub sign-in on hel needs renewing, and tells the user in one line what failed and that the fix is theirs. But the second task message invents a user request ('The person just asked for this round, first') that the transcript never shows, and it tells the task to carry on with the round, which conflicts with stopping the retries. That unsupported claim fails the rule against asserting things the steps don't show. |
| wp-changes-requested-before-wait-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The content is correct: it names the reviewer's requested changes, the pending `tide` check, and that the fix task has the PR, with no merge date. But it runs to four sentences against the rubric's three-sentence limit, and the closing question adds an extra ask of the user. |
| wp-partner-meant-for-other-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner handed the message to 小家 via the partner tool, set no reminder or task itself, asked nothing, and replied with one short Chinese line saying it went to 小家. It claims nothing 小家 has done and uses 你. |
| wp-partner-other-owns-task-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply says plainly that it doesn't know whether the dentist is booked, points to 小家 as the one who handles it, and offers to pass the question on. It states no booking status and opens no task. It does not mention the care home or doctor, but that detail is minor. |
| wp-partner-make-zh | zh | pass | judge: 小家 is created immediately with no questions or task, and the reply uses the user's own framing (管家里的事) and says where to open it (伙伴列表). The extra clause about memory and conversation staying the same is a small overreach, but it doesn't break the rubric. |
| wp-partner-brain-self-en | en | pass | judge: The partner called brain_switch for itself (Mia), opened no task, asked nothing, and gave one short English line saying it will think with Opus from the next message. It did not claim to change Ducter's brain. |
| wp-partner-pass-back-en | en | pass | judge: The partner sent the message to Ducter through the partner tool without opening any task or acting on the release. It replied with one short English line saying it passed the message to Ducter, claimed no answer, and asked nothing. |
| wp-mem-no-cant-lesson-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner reports the failure as a one-time timeout ("这次没查到", "这次") in two short Chinese lines, with no claim that hel or Codex can't browse and nothing saved to memory. Offering a retry as a question is an allowed next step, and no 您 is used. |

| wp-thread-unnamed-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner answers in two short Chinese sentences as someone who remembers: it names Codex and Nov 12, gives the race with the index build and the `await indexReady()` fix. It does not mention searching or paste the conversation, and it uses no guessed cause. |
| wp-promise-kept-zh | zh | pass | judge: Step 1 gives a single Chinese ack naming the action, with no duration or outcome, then opens the task. | wp-babysitter-merge-still-asks-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner did not merge and left the merge decision with the user, which is correct since merging to main is irreversible. | wp-keep-fare-stop-zh | zh | pass | judge: After the user said the ticket was booked, the partner called keep_change (the stop action is implied by the step and the message, though the arg isn't shown) and replied in one short Chinese line saying the 12 月 20 日 monitoring was stopped. The skill description sits in the internal brief, not in user-facing text, so it doesn't count as explaining the skill to the user. |

## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你查一条信息、订一张票。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this machine. For example, I can get a failing test in one of your repos fixed and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요. 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 깨진 테스트 고치기, 파일 정리, 웹 조사, 메일·일정 확인처럼 바로 맡길 수 있는 일을 도와드릴 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 고마워요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を重視し、データが確実に届くよう再送や順序管理を行う。UDPは信頼性より速さを優先し、確認や再送をせず送りっぱなしにする。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local o privada antes de compartirla, por ejemplo para actualizarla con la última `main` y dejar un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir historial que otros usan causa problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的：先握手建立连接，保证数据有序、不丢失、不重复；UDP 是无连接、尽力而为的：直接发，不保证送到，但延迟低、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own private branch before sharing it, so its history reads as a clean series of commits. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rebasing rewrites history others may have built on.
```

- 阈值不再写死在代码里，改成读取配置，默认 8。
- 补测试：默认值是 8；设置成其他值（比如 4）时在第 4 个 L0 文件时触发；非法值的处理有测试覆盖。
- 跑全部测试并报告结果（之前是 212/212，现在应该更多且全过）。
- 报告里写清楚配置项的名字、怎么设置、默认值。

不做的事：
- 不推
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover any committed work that hadn't yet reached the main files.
```

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not run yet</span></div>

<script>
function render(runs) {
  runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.night - a.night; }) : [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!runs.length) {
    empty.style.display = '';
    document.getElementById('latest').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not run yet</span>';
    return;
  }
  empty.style.display = 'none';
  var top = runs[0];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = 'Night ' + top.night + ': ' + top.writes + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + top.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + (top.stalls == null ? '—' : top.stalls);
  runs.forEach(function (r) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">Night ' + r.night + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + (r.stalls == null ? '—' : r.stalls) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + ' /s</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render(ducter.get('runs'));

      var items = load();
      items.splice(i, 1);
      ducter.set(KEY, items);
    });
  });

})();

</details>
</main>
<style>
.k-sum-value{font-size:44px;font-weight:700;color:var(--d-ink)}
</style>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent="¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length? xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    row.innerHTML='<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
    row.querySelector(".k-row-main").textContent=x.what;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-sub") ;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-main").appendChild(sub);
    row.querySelector(".k-row-value").textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? ""+xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||!yuan)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

const CATS = ['交通', '住宿', '餐饮', '其他'];

const esc = s => String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const fmt = n => '¥' + Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('en-US');

function render() {

  $('total').innerHTML =  …  $('cats').innerHTML = CATS.map(c =>
    '<button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="' + (c === cat) + '" data-c="' + c + '">' + c + '</button>').join('');
  ducter.summary(items.length ? items.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(total) : '还没记');
}

function save() {
  ducter.set('expenses', items);
  render();
}

$('cats').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('.k-chip');
  if (!b) return;
  cat = b.dataset.c;
  render();
});

$('save').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const name = $('name').value.trim();
  const amount = Number($('amt').value);
  if (!name || !amount) return;
  items.push({ name, amount, cat, date: $('date').value.trim(), from: '' });
  $('name').value = ''; $('amt').value = ''; $('date').value = '';
  save();
});

$('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!row) return;
  const idx = Number(row.dataset.i);
  ducter.ask('删掉「' + items[idx].name + '」？').then(r => {
    if (r) { items.splice(idx, 1); save(); }
  });
});

ducter.onChange(v => { if (Array.isArray(v)) { items = v; render(); } });
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('expenses')).then(v => { if (Array.isArray(v)) items = v; render(); });
render();

  $('chips').addEventListener('click', ev => {

    if (!b) return;
    cat = b.dataset.c;
    render();
  });

    if (!(amount > 0)) return;
    let date = $('date').value.trim();
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) date = today();

    ducter.set('expenses', [...list(), entry]);
    $('amt').value = ''; $('note').value = ''; $('date').value = '';
    render();
  });

  $('list').addEventListener('click', async ev => {
    const row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    const id = Number(row.dataset.id);
    const e = list().find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!e) return;
    const a = await ducter.ask('Remove ' + fmt(e.amount) + (e.note ? ' (' + e.note + ')' : '') + '?');
    if (a && String(a).toLowerCase() !== 'no') {
      ducter.set('expenses', list().filter(x => x.id !== id));
      render();
    }
  });

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done, it's on your shelf: the four PRs with a tick box each, and the count of what's still waiting drops as you tick them off.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2011 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs, tick each one off as it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<style>
  .k-row { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; cursor:pointer; }
  .k-row input { width:20px; height:20px; flex:none; accent-color: var(--d-accent, #3b6cf6); }
  .k-row-main { flex:1; min-width:0; }
  .k-row.is-landed .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted, #888); }
  .k-row-repo { font-size: 13px; color: var(--d-muted, #888); }
</style>
<script>
  const items = ducter.get("items") || [];
  let landed = {};

  function render() {
    const list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    let done = 0;
    items.forEach(it => {
      const on = !!landed[it.id];
      if (on) done++;
      const row = document.createElement('label');
      row.className = 'k-row' + (on ? ' is-landed' : '');
      row.innerHTML =
        '<input type="checkbox"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '>' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' +
          '<div>' + it.what + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-repo">' + it.repo + ' · ' + it.who + '</div>' +
        '</div>';
      row.querySelector('input').addEventListener('change', e => {
        landed[it.id] = e.target.checked;
        ducter.set('landed', landed);
        render();
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    const left = items.length - done;
    document.getElementById('left').textContent = left;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'of ' + items.length + ' PRs still waiting' + (done ? ', ' + done + ' landed' : '');
    ducter.summary(left + ' waiting');
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); });
  ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); }));
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","what":"Compaction cut","who":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","what":"Retry on 502","who":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","what":"Apps bar","who":"Draft, not yet ready for review"}]}

```

### rich-plain-zh

```
(earlier) user: 看一下 HawDB 上周每天 CI 平均跑多久

(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 我去给 TTL 测试再补一条覆盖正好到期那一刻的用例。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

要求：
- 在 TTL 测试里再加一条用例，使用同一个假时钟，把时间推进到恰好等于过期时刻，断言此时值已过期（或按现有 Cache 的边界约定断言，并在报告里写明约定是什么）。
- 再加一条紧挨着的用例：到期前一毫秒（或一个最小单位）仍然有效，确认边界两侧行为都对。
- 只用假时钟，不要引入真实 sleep 或真实计时。
- 跑一遍整个测试文件，把结果和新用例的名字报告出来。

范围之外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR；改动留在现有分支上提交即可。
```

不在范围内：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。改动留在你自己的分支上提交即可。
